{"forms":[{"form":"dragoons","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["Synonym of dragon (“a type of musket with a short, large-calibre barrel and a flared muzzle, metaphorically exhaling fire like a mythical dragon”)."],"synonyms":[{"tags":["synonym","synonym-of"],"word":"dragon"}],"tags":["historical"],"topics":["engineering","government","military","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","politics","tools","war","weaponry"]},{"glosses":["Originally (historical), a soldier armed with a dragoon musket (noun sense 1.1) who fought both on foot and mounted on a horse; now, a cavalier or horse soldier from a regiment formerly armed with such muskets."],"tags":["broadly"],"topics":["government","military","politics","war"]},{"glosses":["A man with a fierce or unrefined manner, like a dragoon (noun sense 1.2)."],"tags":["broadly"]},{"glosses":["A variety of pigeon, originally a cross between a horseman and a tumbler."]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/dɹəˈɡuːn/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/dɹəˈɡun/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˌdɹæˈɡun/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":282200,"upstream_offset":1410766643,"upstream_record_sha256":"4ada894330bfd755fc690918fb9c9ae86bb6a53af150745363bef1fdb855b4a5","word":"dragoon"}
